## Sheet name: ChemistyGuide
What you're making,Key,What you're doing to make it,Total units,Color Label
"                       Hi! I'm Licks-the-Vials. This is a workflow guide optimized to aspiring chemists for starlight for 120u beakers.
                       
                       I recommend to coordinate with your fellow chemists to divide resources and complete the full workload. 
                       This means claiming categories of chemicals BEFORE you start working, even though we need to be fast!
                       ""Brigmed Chems"" mean a set of the colored reagents for Basic & Airloss Tier 1s and Brute, Burn, Toxin Tier 2s 
                       
                       Your beaker retains heat, there is no need to re-heat it for every recipe, you can heat it quickly with 1u on a hotplate
                       Reductive reactions (bruizine, acetone) also heat your beaker, while reductive reactions (dex) cool it.
                       You can also cool your beaker by adding room tempature, non generated chems from the dispenser, or water/welding fuel.
                       
                       Shorthand Key: (DM) = Dispenser Mix  |  (CMD) = Chem Master Dump  |  (MIM) = Mix in Master  |  (JM) = Jug Mix",,,Copy to a Nano Task paper: 
,,,"This is a workflow guide intended to optimize the speed of producing a base set of useful chemicals.
 
This is optimized for a Silver Beaker (150u capacity) and without dumping Jugs from the Chem Dispenser.

Start with a syringe and siphon 90u of your blood into an empty jug whilst simultaneously dispensing these jugs:

3 Empty, 2 Carbon, 1 Chlorine, 1 Copper, 2 Hydrogen, 1 Iron, 2 Oxygen, 2 Silicon

Master Dump the Plasma vial and Carbon, Hydrogen, Oxygen, Silicon, Copper jugs, then proceed. You can put this paper in your pocket for easy viewing.

Shorthand Key: 
(DM) = Dispenser Mix
(CMD) = Chem Master Dump
(MIM) = Mix in Master  
(JM) = Jug Mix

[head=2]Start Mixing Here[/head]

[bold]DYLO - DM[/bold]
30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen, Twice → Jug

[bold]DERM - DM[/bold]
20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Oxygen, 40 Phosphorus, Twice → Jug & CMD overfill

Prep for Later... - CMD 
90 Lithium, 140 Sodium

30 Derm for Later... DM
5 Silicon, 5 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Phosphorus → CMD

Ammonia - DM
75 Hydrogen, 25 Nitrogen, Twice → CMD

Prep for Later... - CMD 
60 Iron 140 Sugar

[bold]BIC - DM[/bold]
80 Carbon, 20 Oxygen, 20 Sugar, Four Times → CMD

[bold]BRUIZ - MIM[/bold]
50 Bicardine, 50 Sugar, """"50"""" Lithium, Twice → Jug

[bold]ARITH - DM[/bold]
15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Radium, → Jug, 90 Hydrogen → Jug

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

[bold]AMOX - MIM[/bold]
50 Carbon, 50 Iron, 50 Ammonia, Twice

Benzene - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon, Twice → Jug

Hydroxide - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Oxygen → Jug

[bold]PNCT - MIM[/bold]
100 Bicardine, 100 Hydroxide

Ferscilite - MIM
50 Iron, 50 Silicon, Twice

[bold]LEPO - MIM[/bold]
1 Plasma, 50 Fersilicite, 50 Copper, Four Times

Dexaline - MIM
1 Plasma, 40 Oxygen

[bold]DEXP - JM[/bold]
60 Dexalin, 60 Carbon, 60 Iron → Jug

Prep for Later... CMD
200 Chlorine from Jug

Salt - MIM 
100 Sodium, 100 Chlorine

[bold]SALINE - JM[/bold]
40 Salt, 160 Water → Jug

Sodium Carb - MIM
10 Ammonia, 10 Salt, 10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen → Jug

Sodium Hydroxide - MIM
10 Hydrogen, 10 Oxygen, 20 Sodium → Jug

[bold]SIGY - JM[/bold]
40 Sodium Carbonate, 40 Sodium Hydroxide, 20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Sugar, 40 Water

[bold]PYRA - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 60 Dermaline, 60 Carbon → Jug

[bold]INSUZ - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 30 Carbon, 90 Silicon → Jug

[bold]TRIC - DM[/bold]
30 Oxygen, 30 Carbon, 30 Sugar → Jug   30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen → Jug

[head=2]Welding Fuel Chem[/head]

COOL your beaker!
Any cool liquid, Welding Fuel is great for this. Grab at least 75 Fuel and CMD

Prep for Later... CMD 
200 Carbon & Oxygen & Ethnaol from a new Jug or the Dispenser. Dispense 5 additional jugs.

Oil (@520k Oil Ashes) - MIM
50 Welding Fuel, 50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon

WARM your beaker
Specifically use 5u Oil and heat between 400K and 500K (4 seconds on the hot plate)

[bold]DIPH - JM[/bold]
20 Ammonia, 60 Ethanol, 40 Oil, 40 Salt → Jug THEN 40 Carbon → Jug 

Ephedrine - MIM
10 Oil, 5 Ammonia, 5 Ethanol, 10 Hydrogen, 10 Sugar

Acetone - MIM
25 Oil, 25 Welding Fuel, 25 Oxygen

Phenol - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 25 Carbon, 25 Oxygen

Hydroxide - MIM
25 Hydrogen, 25 Oxygen

50 Phenol, 50 Acetone, 50 Chlorine, 50 Hydroxide → Jug

[bold]OCULINE - MIM[/bold]
20 Salt, 20 Blood, 20 Hydrogen, 20 Oxygen 

[head=2]Cryo Chem+[/head]

Prep for later... CMD
150 Water (if not from earlier), 30 Phosphorus, 30 Radium, 60 Lithium, 10 Sugar

Tricordine - DM
10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Sugar, THEN 10 Silicon, 10 Potassium, 10 Nitrogen → MD

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

Dexalin - MIM
1 Plasma, 50 Oxygen, Twice

50 Dexalin, 50 Oxygen, 50 Water, Thrice (Jug 180)

## Sheet name: GoldBeakerGuide
,,"This guide is made for those that prefer using the chem master and assumes you are using the golden beaker (240u) or are mixing inside jugs. hopefully i made this new player friendly enough that everyone can follow it easily. If a recipe requires heat, preheat your beaker with 1u of plasma inside for a few seconds and empty it before starting.","tips and tricks:
1) your beaker retains heat, there is no need to re-heat it for every recipe.
2) filling your beaker with water/fuel from a tank or sink cools it to room temperature.
3) expanding reactions (dex) cool your beaker down, reducing reactions (amoxla) heat it.
4) don't forget to pull chemicals from the dispenser even when you don't need them yet.
5) coordinate, pick a category and let your fellow chemists know that you are making it.
6) alt click the master/dispenser to quickly pull the beaker out
7) experiment, everyone will have their prefered method, once you've learned the ropes try not to blindly follow mine"
,,"Start the shift by pulling 200 iron, 100 sugar, 800 oxygen, 600 hydrogen 600 carbon and 100 sillicon from the dispenser, this can be skipped if you wish but it generally helps speed you up. 
Continue by pulling out the jugs from the dispenser and putting their contents into the master by dragging and dropping the jugs onto it. 
Take the following jugs from the chemvend depending on what you will be making: 
Airloss:  1 iron. Brutes: 1 carbon, 1 sugar.

## Sheet name: Plumbing
Read the full guide of starlight plumbing in-game first. The rest of this guide assumes you have this knowledge

Prioritize making chemicals before plumbing anything.
"If one is not present, create and link a system to a Plumbing Fridge and Chem Master after the initial set of chemicals are created. From there, make the refiils manually before setting up complicated automation."
"It is typically far faster for you to manually create chems to refill fridges than setup a plumbing system. Unless you plan to leave chemistry, make the refills manually until you have free time."

Key understandings:
"Plumbing is Pull-based, meaning reagents do not flow through pipes like air in gas pipes. Instead reagents sit in the machines and buffers and are pulled at 20u / 2 seconds"
"The containers in the machines can get clogged very easily by producing too many of the incorrect reagent. Using filters avoids this, but keep in mind you cant chain a filter on a filter. Chaining filters makes the second filter only see the reagents in the buffer of the first filter, which is prone to clogging with base reagents."

Configuration for one reactor system that makes Bic and Derm for menders (clogs),Chems Made,Temp Set
"5 Carbon, 3 Oxygen, 1 Sugar, 2 Phosphorus, 1 Silicon",Bic + Derm,293K
,,,Tip! Use this shorthand
Configurations for a system using reactors that have multiple output chemicals (prone to clogs),,,when setting up reactors!

"2 bic, 1 hydrogen, 1 oxygen, 1 kelotane, 1 Lepo, 1 Silicon",Punct + Insuz,450K
"1 Plasma, 3 carbon, 5 Iron, 2 Silicon, 4 Copper, 2 Oxygen",Dexp + Lepo,350K
"1 Bic, 1 Hydrogen, 2 Carbon, 1 Lepo, 1 Derm",Lace + Pyra,560K

Configurations for a system using a reactor for each output chemical (does not ever clog),,,as = plasma
"20 bic, 20 hydrogen, 20 carbon",Lace,350K,xy = oxygen
"20 bic, 10 hydrogen, 10 oxygen",Punct,350K,ar = carbon & bicardine
"40 carbon, 10 oxygen, 10 sugar",Bic,293K,ga = sugar
"10 carbon, 20 oxygen, 20 phosphorus, 10 silicon",Derm,293K,hy = hydrogen
"10 plasma, 30 carbon, 30 iron, 20 oxygen",Dexp,293K,er = copper & dermaline
"1 plasma, 20 iron, 20 silicon, 40 copper",Lepo,350K,ro = iron
"20 derm, 20 carbon, 20 lepo",Pyra,560K,us = phosphorus
"10 carbon, 30 silicon, 20 lepo",Insuz,450K,si = silicon
,,,le = lepo
Ideally filter what you put into the fridge to prevent contraband chemicals like Phosphorus and Silicon from entering it.
Ensure you have an input in the system to add the initial plasma necessary for Lepo and Dex+ reactors.

"Further expanding the system and manually adding chemicals like Salt, Lithium, Nitrogen and Potassium allows for nearly all useful chemicals to be made. This is not recommended as it is far faster to create those by hand using the dispenser and chem master instead."

Creating visible sinks for Bic and Derm near the cryostasis beds is recommended for new doctors to easily understand how to refill and use automenders.

"A round-start system to connect to a fridge to create Bic, Derma, Lace and Punct (not optimal)"

Optimal plumbing setups will require you to use the synths as the piping. 
The smallest 8 reactor builds only need 4x5 tiles:
├ X ┼ X ┤                 ├ X ┼ X ┤
├ X ┼ X ┤                 ┴ ┴ ┴ ┴ ┴
├ X ┼ X ┤                 ^ Bic and Derm
├ X ┼ X ┤
"Due to the nature of this, you can also have the middle piping ┼ effectively by a walkable path to reach all synths and reactors"
"However, station layouts often do not have room for this. Some layout options include:"

## Sheet name: Botany
"A botanist serves more than just Chef, they also need to make plants for orders, and medical reagents for medical, and biomass for science and medical."
"This guide unveils deeper game mechanics that are typically intended to be discovered through roleplay, you have been warned!"
"Read the full guide of starlight botany in-game first. The rest of this guide assumes you have this knowledge
"

,,,,,,,"We dont have this yet, but look forward "
,,,,,,,to the analyzer that demystifies plant stats!

,,,,,,,"This will expose some stats the plant has, "
,,,,,,,greatly removing the RNG aspect in Botany!

,,,,,,,"Using this tool you can reliably mutate,"
,,,,,,,"manage health, and swab chemicals to plants"
,,,,,,,without waiting to harvest or for alarms

Here are some game mechanics to keep in mind:

Examining (Shift Click) seeds shows the Yield (how many plants are produced) and Potency (the plant size + quantity of reagents inside it)
Composting adds nutrients puportional to the potency and adds the chemicals in the plant to the tray to metabolize overtime,,,,,,,                    Tip! You can compost 15u+ 
Hydroponic Trays process chemicals at 1u every 3 seconds.,,,,,,,"                    of Flour, then add water to "
"Sampling seeds damage the plant, and the seeds inherit the health and traits of the original plant.",,,,,,,                    the tray to create Dough for
Biomassing plants converts the Nutriment chemical at a 1:1 rate to biomass.,,,,,,,                    the Chef while upping your 
"Animals breed occasionaly when not hungry, thirsty, incapacitated, gestating, an infant, or at population capacity within a 3 tile radius. ",,,,,,,                    tray's nutri and water levels
"Chickens cap at 12, Cows at 6, Pigs at 8"

MUTATIONS
"Mutations occur every 15 seconds as mutation energy (a hidden stat up cappped at 25) is spent. Using mutagen adds 1 mutation energy per 1u, unless multiplied"
"Plants start with a mutation rate of 1. This is a per-plant stat, it cannot be swabbed or inherited. Mutation rate multiplies the effect of mutation chemicals up to 3x"
"Mutations all have a base chance to roll that is multiplied by the mutation energy. Every mutation is rolled in the 15 second batch, then mutation energy resets to 0."

SWABBING
Swabs copy the traits of the plant it swabs (even if it is dead) then has a 50% chance to paste each of the copied traits to the next plant it swabs.
"Because swabs copy the traits of the plant every use, it's recommended to use each swab a single time to preserve the initial plant traits you want to copy."
Swabing useful chemicals to plants that are innately multi-harvest like lemon/lime/bananas is recommended to decrease overall harvest time
"Transformation through mutation adds (not replaces) guaranteed chemicals, allowing you to swab yield, potency, and useful chemicals prior to transforming."

"Prioritize medicinal and flour chemicals, and cargo-related plants while creating a healthy stockpile of biomass:"

## Sheet name: Notes
What is Master Dump?,Why?
"Click and drag jugs or beakers directly into a Chem Master to quickly move the liquids inside.
 
It works like a Janitor would use a Drain to dump liquids.",This makes it easier to maintain your understanding of what beaker is hot and cold by not ejecting the hot beaker from the Chem Master

How does heat work?,What's a reductive mixture?
"Heat doesn't change over time and a beaker will maintain its heat indefinitely until liquid is put in a beaker or there is a reductive mixture. 

Moving liquids between beakers will maintain the heat in the new beaker as long as it is empty. If the beaker already had liquid then the temp will combine between the liquids that are mixed together.","When you make a chemical that reduces the overall units of the liquid, the heat increases. Conceptually, the same amount of heat is just being shared among fewer units; an exothermic reaction. 

However, this change of temp can occur in the Chem Master and be maintained, making beakers really hot really easily over the course of the chem order, starting with Benzene."

"Note this is for beaker usage. Jug mixing is also very fast, stop at line 12 and simply put the reagents in different jugs, 30u at a time."
Advanced Diph Notes:,Why?
Start with two empty beakers,We'll use one beaker to maintain a heat between 377K and 540K while the other mixes stuff together and changes heat.
Make beaker one cold,You can reset by dispensing room temp liquid into an empty beaker
Put in 5u of any liquid (oil) in beaker one,Oil can make it easy to tell if it's too hot when you dont have goggles
Heat for 4-5 seconds ,"If you ash the oil around 540K you've gone too far, reset the process."
Transfer the liquid from beaker one to beaker two,"Transfering the liquid will make both beakers have the same heat, regardless of their previous heat value."
"Put beaker two in the Chem Master and mix Diph (20u oil, carbon, salt, dieth, ethanol)

Ensure the last 20u that completes the mixture is put in all at once.","Because this is a reductive mixture it will heat the beaker and ash the remaining oil (which turns into charcoal) if it is not all mixed at the same time

If you have an odd mixture as a result of putting in less than 20u, empty all the chemicals back in the master and continue the process. You'll end with less Diph."
Dump the Diph in the Chem Master
Eject beaker two and hold in hand,We'll need to use beaker one to reset the heat below 540K again
Put the empty beaker one in the Chem Master and Extract any liquid,"The Chem Master will maintain the heat of the beaker even if it started with no liquids. 
We can pull this out to normalize the temp again"
Eject beaker one and transfer the liquid from beaker one to beaker two
Repeat the process of mixing Diph with beaker two until you're happy.

## Sheet name: DocsOld

Chem for Kobolds Introduction,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Introduction[/head][/color]

Chemicals are powerful reagents that cause a variety of effects when metabolized. This edition of the Chemistry for Kobold series introduces various concepts and guidelines to use when creating chemicals.

[color=#0091ff][bold]STANDARD EXPECTATIONS[/bold][/color]
Every Medbay is unique; always consult with seniors. 
Most will expect the following chemicals to be made:

[bold]Basics (within 10 minutes):
• [color=#ffaa00]BIC        | Brute[/color]
• [color=#216b85]DERM   | Burn (except Caustic)[/color]
• [color=#6745c6]DYLO    | Poison[/color]
• [color=#bd5902]ARITH   | Radiation[/color]
• [color=#0064c8]SALINE | Bloodlevel[/color]
• [color=#4da0bd]DEXP     | Airloss + Bloodloss[/color]
• [color=#89f77f]AMOX   | Avali Airloss + Bloodloss[/color]

Advanced (within 30 minutes):
• [color=#ff3636]BRUIZ   | Blunt[/color] 
• [color=#788282]LACE     | Slash[/color] 
• [color=#b9bf93]PUNC    | Pierce[/color] 
• [color=#ff7db5]LEPO     | Cold [/color] 
• [color=#aa4308]PYRA     | Heat [/color] 
• [color=#8147ff]INSUZ   | Shock [/color] 
• [color=#e0a5b9]SIGY      | Caustic [/color] 

Cryochemicals:
• [color=#0091ff]CRYOX  | Cure-all (except cellular) when alive[/color]
• [color=#32cd32]DOXA    | Cellular when alive[/color]
• [color=#86a5bd]NECRO  | Cure-all (except cellular) when dead[/color]

Others, as requested:
• [color=#64ffe6]DIPH     | Poison [/color]
• [color=#ba7d7d]TRANX  | Stop Bleed[/color] 
• [color=#2d5708]ETHYL   | -Dizzy + Drunk[/color] 
• [color=#27870a]HALOP  | -Stims + Causes Sleep[/color] 
• [color=#efefef]EPI         | Cure-all when critical [/color] 
• [color=#00e5ff]TRIC      | Cure-all when healthy (<50 Damage)[/color] 
• [color=#999999]OCULINE    | Eyesight[/color] 
• [color=#4b5154]CHARCOAL | Clean Blood [/color] 
[/bold]
During this, specialized medications like botany and combat may be requested. See the other Chemistry for Kobold editions for the list of those chemicals. 

[color=#0091ff][bold]METHODS OF MIXING CHEMICALS[/bold][/color]
Most chemists will [bold]prioritize speed[/bold]. Depending on the mixture requirements, the method of mixing should change between using the Chem Master, Chem Dispenser, or within empty Jugs to achieve results.

Many Chemists elect to dump every chemical into the Chem Master and sort the chemicals alphabetically. This is OK, especially when working collaboratively, though the risks are high and [bold]all is lost[/bold] should the master be destroyed with improper mixtures or external forces.

As such, the Chemistry for Kobold editions [bold]recommend to maintain the Chem Dispenser[/bold] through refills, and dispense chemicals as-needed for a workflow.

Certain mixtures like Phal, Arith, Dylo, Bic, Derm are[bold] easy and fast to make[/bold] using the Chem Dispenser. Otherwise Jug mixing and Chem Master mixing is faster and easier when using Heat Manipulation.

[color=#0091ff][bold]HOW HEAT MANIPULATION WORKS[/bold][/color]
[bold]Heat doesn't change over time[/bold] and Beakers, Vials, Bottles and Syringes (containers) will maintain their heat indefinitely [bold]until a new liquid is put in[/bold] or there is a reductive mixture. 

This means putting room temperature water in any [bold]empty[/bold] container will cause container to be at room temperature even when emptied.

Putting new liquid into a container will combine the temperature of the mixed liquids, unless it is done using the Chem Master.

[bold]The Chem Master dispenses chemicals at the same tempature as the container inside it.[/bold]

Because of this, many Chemists will [bold]heat 1u of liquid in a `Hot` beaker[/bold] to 1000K+ in [bold]10 seconds[/bold], then keep the `Hot` beaker in the Chem Master for making heat-based mixtures.

[color=#0091ff][bold]WHAT IS A REDUCTIVE MIXTURE?[/bold][/color]
When making chemicals that reduce the overall units of the liquid, the [bold]heat increases.[/bold] 

A common example of this is Benzene, where 2 units of liquid become 1 in the mixture.

This change of temperature can occur anywhere, [bold]including in the Chem Master.[/bold] This is why a beaker can be accidentally overheated when making chemicals like Diph. This can also be utilized to gradually heat up a beaker from 310K to 1000K+ in a Chem Master.

[color=#0091ff][bold]WHAT IS A CATALYST?[/bold][/color]
A catalyst is sometimes necessary for certain chemicals. The catalyst reagent [bold]is not consumed[/bold] in the mixture and any amount, such as 1u will work.

[color=#0091ff][bold]HOW TO COLLABORATE WELL[/bold][/color]
Multiple Chemists work together and need to share resources. [bold]Ensure constrained resources are correctly distributed[/bold] for the work. Based on recommended units, each common category of chemicals [bold]need[/bold] the following Chem Vends:

• Air & Brutes | 400 Carbon, 200 Oxygen
• Burns & Toxin | 400 Carbon, 200 Oxygen, 200 Silicon
• Botany | 200 Radium, 200 Chlorine, 200 Phosphorus

When resources are constrainted, use machines to create additional chemicals, order a Chem Vend restock, or order Chemical Crates.

Chem for Kobolds Basics,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Basics[/head][/color]This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

These basics should be made within 5 minutes or less when practiced.

[signature]
[bold]:[color=#ffaa00]BIC|BRT|15/5u|15OD+MixBRT
• 4 Carbon, 1 Oxygen, 1 Sugar[/bold][/color]
Use 15u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#216b85]DERM|BRNnoCaus|15/5u|10OD
• 1 Carbon, 1 Silicon, 2 Oxygen, 2 Phosphorus[/bold][/color]
Use 15u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#6853a3]DYLO|Poison|10/5u|20OD
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen[/bold][/color]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#bd5902]ARITH|Rads|30/5u|+BRTDmg
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen, 3 Radium, 6 Hydrogen[/bold][/color]
Use 5u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 60u thrice.

[signature]
[bold],[color=#0064c8]SALINE|Bloodlevel|15%/5u
• 1 Chlorine, 1 Sodium, Heat to 370K, 4 Water[/bold][/color]
Use 20u on the Dispenser, get the Sodium and Chlorine then Hotplate the mixture. When Salt is created, dump in jug and fill jug with 160u water.

[signature]
[bold],[color=#519fbb]DEXP|Asphx+Bldls|35/5u|25OD
• 1 Dexalin, 1 Carbon, 1 Iron[/bold][/color]
[italic]Dexalin: 1 Oxygen, 1 Plasma (Catalyst)[/italic]
To make Dexalin, use 1u Plasma as a Catalyst with 50u Oxygen. Remove the excess and deposit 65u of the required reagents into a jug.

To ensure a quick workflow, prepare to use liquid plasma and at least 100 Carbon, 110 Oxygen, 100 Hydrogen, 100 Sugar, 150 Iron.

[signature]
[bold],[color=#0064c8]SALINE|Bloodlevel|15%/5u
• 1 Chlorine, 1 Sodium, Heat to 370K, 4 Water[/bold][/color]
Use 20u on the Dispenser, get the Sodium and Chlorine then Hotplate the mixture. When Salt is created, dump in jug and fill jug with 160u water.

[signature]
[bold],[color=#519fbb]DEXP|Asphx+Bldls|35/5u|25OD
• 1 Dexalin, 1 Carbon, 1 Iron[/bold][/color]
[italic]Dexalin: 1 Oxygen, 1 Plasma (Catalyst)[/italic]
To make Dexalin, use 1u Plasma as a Catalyst with 50u Oxygen. Remove the excess and plasma and deposit 65u of the required reagents into a jug. 

[signature]
[bold],[color=#89f77f]AMOX|AmniaAIR|30/5u|25OD
• 1 Ammonia, 1 Carbon, 1 Iron, Heat to 540K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Ammonia: 3 Hydrogen, 1 Nitrogen[/italic]
Use 15u on the Dispenser to create Ammonia, then dump it in the Chem Master. Quickly mix 30u of the required reagents twice in a different `Hot` beaker.

[signature]

[italic]Sulfuric Acid: 1 Sulfur, 2 Oxygen, 1 Hydrogen
Inapprovaline: 1 Carbon, 1 Oxygen, 1 Sugar[/italic]
Use 20u on the Dispenser or Chem Master, Create Sulfuric acid and dump into a Jug. Then create Inapp and dump into the same jug. Finally, deposit the remaining sugar needed.

Chem for Kobolds Brutes,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Brutes[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

These are the basic and advanced chemicals in the Brute category.

To ensure a quick workflow, prepare to use at least 600 Carbon, 200 Oxygen, 200 Hydrogen, 200 Sugar.

[signature]
[bold]:[color=#ffaa00]BIC|BRT|15/5u|15OD+MixBRT
• 4 Carbon, 1 Oxygen, 1 Sugar[/bold][/color]
As a prerequisite for other brutes, at least 450 of bic is necessary. Create using the Chem Master, then parse out 50u into two jugs, 100u into two jugs, and the remainder into a final jug.

[signature]
[bold]:[color=#ff3636]BRUIZ|Blunt|45/5u|10OD+BRT
• 1 Bicardine, 1 Sugar, .9 Lithium[/bold][/color]
To avoid making Razorium, the mixture must combine [bold]in one action[/bold] in a single container. Use the Dispenser and retrieve 50u Sugar and 45u Lithium. Dump into the jugs that have 50u of Bic in a single action. Then combine the Bruiz jugs.

[signature]
[bold]:[color=#788282]LACE|Slash|40/5u|12OD+BRT
• 1 Bicardine, 1 Benzene, Heat to 335K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Benzene: Hydrogen, Carbon, Heat to 310K[/italic]
To avoid making Razorium, the mixture must combine [bold]in one action[/bold] in a single container. Create Benzene with 50u Carbon and Hydrogen twice in the Chem Master. Then dump the 100u of Benzene in the 100u Bic Jug.

[signature]
[bold]:[color=#b9bf93]PNCT|Pierce|50/5u|11OD+BRT
• 1 Bicardine, 1 Hydroxide, Heat to 325K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Hydroxide: Hydrogen, Oxygen, Heat to 310K[/italic]
To avoid making Razorium, the mixture must combine [bold]in one action[/bold] in a single container. Create Hydroxide with 50u Oxygen and Hydrogen in the Chem Master. Then dump the 100u of Hydroxide in the 100u Bic Jug.

Chem for Kobolds Burns,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Burns[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

These are the basic and advanced chemicals in the Burns category.

To ensure a quick workflow, prepare to use liquid plasma and at least 200 Carbon, 200 Oxygen, 200 Hydrogen, 200 Copper, 200 Ethanol, 200 Iron, 200 Silicon, 200 Phosphorus.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#216b85]DERM|BRNnoCaus|15/5u|10OD
• 1 Carbon, 1 Silicon, 2 Oxygen, 2 Phosphorus[/bold][/color]
Use 15u on the Dispenser to fill a beaker to 90u thrice. Dump excess Derma into the Chem Master for Pyra.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#ff7db5]LEPO|Cold|40/5u|<20kgOD
• 1 Copper, 1 Fersilicite, Plasma (Catalyst)[/bold][/color]
[italic]Fersilicite: 1 Iron, 1 Silicon, Heat to 310K[/italic]
Mix 50u of Iron and Silicon twice. Then use 1u of Plasma as a Catalyst and mix uneven batches of Copper and Fersilcite until 400u of Lepo is created. Keep extra Lepo for the other advanced burn mixes.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#964e24]PYRA|Heat|50/5u|15OD
• 1 Carbon, 1 Dermaline, 1 Leporazine, Heat to 540K[/bold][/color]
Due to previous mixes, retrieve 30u of each reagent in a `Hot` beaker, then deposit in jug.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#9668fc]INSU|Shock|30/5u|12OD+<20kg
• 1 Silicon, 1 Kelotane, 1 Leporazine, Heat to 433K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Kelotane: 1 Carbon, 1 Silicon[/italic]
Using 30u, Create 60u of Kelo and then retrieve the required reagents in a `Hot` beaker, then deposit in jug.

[signature]
[bold].[color=#e0a5b9]SIGY|Caustic|25/5u|16OD
• 1 Sodium Carbonate, 1 Sodium Hydroxide, 1 Kelotane, 1 Water, 1 Sugar, Heat to 370K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Ammonia: 3 Hydrogen, 1 Nitrogen
Sodium Carbonate: 1 Ammonia, 1 Salt, 1 Carbon, 1 Oxygen
Sodium Hydroxide: 1 Hydrogen, 1 Oxygen, 2 Sodium, Heat to 310K[/italic]
To cleanly mix 160u, use 5u in the Dispenser to make 20u of Ammonia. Then dump in the Chem Master. Then use 10u in the Dispenser to retrieve 40 Sodium and 20 Chlorine for the Chem Master. If not already retrieved, dump 40u of Sugar and Water in the Chem Master. 

Using a different `Hot` beaker in the Chem Master, use 20u to mix Carbon and Silicon for Kelo. Then use 10u to mix the reagents for Sodium Carbonate and Sodium Hydroxide. With the Chem Master set to `Newest Reagent` instead of Alphabetical, use 20u to easily find, and mix the reagents for Sigy twice.

Chem for Kobolds Toxin,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Toxin[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

These are the basic and advanced chemicals in the Toxin category.

To ensure a quick workflow, find and prepare 200u Welding Fuel, 200u Carbon, 200 Hydrogen, 200 Nitrogen, 200 Silicon, 200 Potassium.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#6853a3]DYLO|Poison|10/5u|20OD
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen[/bold][/color]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#bd5902]ARITH|Rads|30/5u|+BRTDmg
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen, 3 Radium, 6 Hydrogen[/bold][/color]
Use 5u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 60u thrice.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#c8ff75]PHAL|Cell|10/5u|+RadsDmg
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen, 2 Phosphorus, 2 Chlorine, 5 Radium, 6 Ethanol[/bold][/color]
Use 5u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[signature]
[bold]`[color=#64ffe6]DIPH|Poison|30/5u
• 1 Diethylamine, 1 Oil, 1 Salt, 1 Carbon, 1 Ethanol, Heat to 377K[/bold][/color]
[italic]Ammonia: 3 Hydrogen, 1 Nitrogen
Diethylamine: 1 Ammonia, 1 Ethanol
Oil: 1 Welding Fuel, 1 Hydrogen, 1 Carbon, Must be COLD[/italic]
Making excessive Diph is useful for Necrosol. To cleanly mix 270u, use 15u in the dispenser to make 60 Ammonia. Then dump in the Chem Master and mix with Ethanol to make 90u of Diethylamine. Then use 25u in the Dispenser to retrieve 50 Sodium and 50 Chlorine for the Chem Master. 

[bold]For new chemists,[/bold] use a `Hot` beaker to create Salt with the Sodium and Chlorine and dump in the Chem Master. Then use a COLD beaker, use 20u to mix the required reagents and place on a hot plate. Repeat with COLD beakers until reagents are depleted.

[bold]For more experienced chemists,[/bold] using a COLD beaker, use 30u to mix the reagents for Oil. Take only 5u of Oil and place it on a hot plate for 3 seconds. This should make a `Warm` beaker above 377K, but below 520K, before the oil ashes. If the oil ashes, try again with a COLD beaker.

Using the `Warm` beaker, use 50u to mix Chlorine and Sodium for Salt, then prepare three empty jugs. Ensure the transfer amount of the `Warm` beaker is set to 100u and is empty. Retrieve 30u of Oil, Ethanol and Diethylamine to deposit 90u of reagents in each jug. Separately retrieve 30u of Salt and Carbon to deposit 60u of the remaining reagents in each jug [bold]in a single action[/bold]. This makes 90u of Diph in each jug. Combine the jugs and use the excess for Necrosol.

Chem for Kobolds Cryochemical,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Cryochemical[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

Cryochemicals are powerful reagents but require the body to be cold, usually <213K or <150K. Typically, this is achieved through Cryogenic technology. However, freezing the body in space or cold environments also works. Some reagents only metabolize when the body is alive or dead.

To ensure a quick workflow, prepare to use plasma and 400 Oxygen, 200 Water, at least 70 Diphen. 

The process for Diphen creation is described in the Chemistry for Kobolds | Toxin. Extra Oxygen is also easily created using an Electrolizer with Water when available.

[signature]
[bold]^[color=#0091ff]CRYOX|ALLnoCELL|<213K|ALIVE
• 1 Dexalin, 1 Oxygen, 1 Water[/bold][/color]
[italic]Dexalin: 1 Oxygen, Plasma (Catalyst)[/italic]
To make Dexalin, use 1u Plasma as a Catalyst with 50u Oxygen thrice. Removing the plasma and then using 30u, mix the required reagents four times to prepare for other cryochemicals.

[signature]
[bold]^[color=#32cd32]DOXA|CELL|5/1u|<213K|ALIVE
• 3 Cryox, 1 Radium, 1 Phosphorus, 1 Chlorine[/bold][/color]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, mix Radium, Phosphorus, and Chlorine first. Deposit the Mutagen in a jug, then retrieve 90u of Cryox and mix in the same Jug.

[signature]
[bold]^[color=#86a5bd]NECR|ALLnoRAD|5/1u|DEAD
• 1 Diphen, 1 Desoxye, 1 Blood, 1 Cryox[/bold][/color]
[italic]Diethylamine: 3 Hydrogen, 1 Nitrogen, 1 Ethanol
Oil: Welding Fuel, Hydrogen, Carbon
Ephedrine: Oil, Diethylamine, Hydrogen, Sugar, Must be COLD
Desoxye: Ephedrine, Carbon, Iodine, Phosphorus, Heat to 370K[/italic]
Use 5u on the Dispenser, add 5 Nitrogen, 15 Sugar, 20 Carbon, 35 Hydrogen, 20u Ethanol in a COLD beaker. Dump the mix in the Chem Master. 
Use a `Hot` beaker to mix 20u of the required reagents for Desoxye. Dump in the Chem Master.
Collect at least 90u of Blood in a container and dump it in the Chem Master.
Use a `Hot` beaker to mix 25u of the required reagents for Necro until the reagents are depleted.

[signature]
[bold]^[color=#850063]CHRON|RAD|10/1u|<213K|DEAD
• 2 Hyroalin, 2 Arithrazine, 2 Cryoxadone, 1 Necrosol[/bold][/color]
Use 30u of Necro from before in a Jug, Use 15u on the Dispenser, mix Nitrogen, Potassium, and Silicon, Radium three times then add to the jug. Dispense 30 Hydrogen and add 60u of Cryox and dump in the Jug twice.

[signature]
[bold]^[color=#86caf7]ARCR|BRT+BRN|>200dmg|DEAD
• 1 Lithium, 1 Tricordine, 1 Cryoxadone[/bold][/color]
[italic]Tricordine: Carbon, Oxygen, Sugar & Silicon, Nitrogen, Potassium[/italic]
Use 15u on the Dispenser, add Carbon, Oxygen, Sugar, THEN Silicon, Potassium, Nitrogen in that order. Add to a jug. Dispense 60u Lithium and add to jug. Add 60u of Cryox from earlier to the jug.

[color=#eeeeee]
If you wish to download this content to your PDA, search this link: https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1ExvhhgdQMwxDBjOEoU6wGEOixlurMb_ECHWumff4kIU"
Chem for Kobolds Botany,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Botany[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

Botanists often create useful reagents like Aloe, Stella, and Omni. It's mutually beneficial to provide Botanist reagents to aid in their work.

• 1 Dexalin, 1 Oxygen, 1 Water[/bold][/color]
[italic]Dexalin: 1 Oxygen, Plasma (Catalyst)[/italic]

Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

• 1 Nitrogen, 1 Phosphorus, 1 Potassium, Radium (Catalyst)[/bold][/color]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u of the required reagents and add 1u of the catalyst after.

[signature]
[bold]~[color=#674ea7]L4Z|preps4muta per plant
• 1 Radium, 1 Phosphorus, 1 Chlorine[/bold][/color]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u twice.

[bold]~[color=#00ff00]MUTA|mutates per 1u
• 1 Silicon, 1 Potassium, 1 Nitrogen, 2 Phosphorus, 2 Chlorine, 5 Radium, 6 Ethanol[/bold][/color]

[signature]
[bold]~[color=#c8ff75]PHAL|Remove Unviable/5u
• 1 Nitrogen, 1 Phosphorus, 1 Potassium, Sulfuric Acid (Catalyst)[/bold][/color]
[italic]Sulfuric Acid: 1 Sulfur, 2 Oxygen, 1 Hydrogen[/italic]
Use 30u on the Dispenser, fill the beaker to 90u of the required reagents and add the catalyst after. To create the catalyst, Use 1u on the Dispenser and mix Sulfur, Oxygen and Hydrogen once.

Chem for Kobolds Combat,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Combat[/head][/color]
This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

Occasionally, it's necessary to prepare chemical mixtures to aid in combat. Most species can metabolize two reagents at the same time

[signature]
[bold]**********COMBAT**********
• 1 Dermaline, 1 Kelotane[/bold][/color]
[italic]Kelotane: 1 Carbon, 1 Silicon
Derma: 1 Carbon, 1 Silicon, 2 Phosphorus, 2 Oxygen[/italic]

[signature]
[bold]*[color=#b9bf93]PNCTX|PierceBld|30/5u|15OD
• 1 Lace, 1 Pyrazine[/bold][/color]
[italic]Lace: 1 Bicardine, 1 Benzene, Heat to 335K
Pyra: 1 Carbon, 1 Dermaline, 1 Leporazine, Heat to 540K[/italic]

Licks Order Task,"This is a workflow guide intended to optimize the speed of producing a base set of useful chemicals.
 
This is optimized for a Silver Beaker (150u capacity) and without dumping Jugs from the Chem Dispenser.

Start with a syringe and siphon 90u of your blood into an empty jug whilst simultaneously dispensing these jugs:

3 Empty, 2 Carbon, 1 Chlorine, 1 Copper, 2 Hydrogen, 1 Iron, 2 Oxygen, 2 Silicon

Master Dump the Plasma vial and Carbon, Hydrogen, Oxygen, Silicon, Copper jugs, then proceed. You can put this paper in your pocket for easy viewing.

Shorthand Key: 
(DM) = Dispenser Mix
(CMD) = Chem Master Dump
(MIM) = Mix in Master  
(JM) = Jug Mix

[head=2]Start Mixing Here[/head]

[bold]DYLO - DM[/bold]
30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen, Twice → Jug

[bold]DERM - DM[/bold]
20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Oxygen, 40 Phosphorus, Twice → Jug & CMD overfill

Prep for Later... - CMD 
90 Lithium, 140 Sodium

30 Derm for Later... DM
5 Silicon, 5 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Phosphorus → CMD

Ammonia - DM
75 Hydrogen, 25 Nitrogen, Twice → CMD

Prep for Later... - CMD 
60 Iron 140 Sugar

[bold]BIC - DM[/bold]
80 Carbon, 20 Oxygen, 20 Sugar, Four Times → CMD

[bold]BRUIZ - MIM[/bold]
50 Bicardine, 50 Sugar, """"50"""" Lithium, Twice → Jug

[bold]ARITH - DM[/bold]
15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Radium, → Jug, 90 Hydrogen → Jug

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

[bold]AMOX - MIM[/bold]
50 Carbon, 50 Iron, 50 Ammonia, Twice

Benzene - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon, Twice → Jug

Hydroxide - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Oxygen → Jug

[bold]LACE - MIM[/bold]
100 Bicardine, 100 Benzene

[bold]PNCT - MIM[/bold]
100 Bicardine, 100 Hydroxide

Ferscilite - MIM
50 Iron, 50 Silicon, Twice

[bold]LEPO - MIM[/bold]
1 Plasma, 50 Fersilicite, 50 Copper, Four Times

Dexaline - MIM
1 Plasma, 40 Oxygen

[bold]DEXP - JM[/bold]
60 Dexalin, 60 Carbon, 60 Iron → Jug

Prep for Later... CMD
200 Chlorine from Jug

Salt - MIM 
100 Sodium, 100 Chlorine

[bold]SALINE - JM[/bold]
40 Salt, 160 Water → Jug

Sodium Carb - MIM
10 Ammonia, 10 Salt, 10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen → Jug

Sodium Hydroxide - MIM
10 Hydrogen, 10 Oxygen, 20 Sodium → Jug

[bold]SIGY - JM[/bold]
40 Sodium Carbonate, 40 Sodium Hydroxide, 20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Sugar, 40 Water

[bold]PYRA - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 60 Dermaline, 60 Carbon → Jug

[bold]INSUZ - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 30 Carbon, 90 Silicon → Jug

[bold]TRIC - DM[/bold]
30 Oxygen, 30 Carbon, 30 Sugar → Jug   30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen → Jug

[head=2]Welding Fuel Chem[/head]

COOL your beaker!
Any cool liquid, Welding Fuel is great for this. Grab at least 75 Fuel and CMD

Prep for Later... CMD 
200 Carbon & Oxygen & Ethnaol from a new Jug or the Dispenser. Dispense 5 additional jugs.

Oil (@520k Oil Ashes) - MIM
50 Welding Fuel, 50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon

WARM your beaker
Specifically use 5u Oil and heat between 400K and 500K (4 seconds on the hot plate)

[bold]DIPH - JM[/bold]
20 Ammonia, 60 Ethanol, 40 Oil, 40 Salt → Jug THEN 40 Carbon → Jug 

Ephedrine - MIM
10 Oil, 5 Ammonia, 5 Ethanol, 10 Hydrogen, 10 Sugar

Acetone - MIM
25 Oil, 25 Welding Fuel, 25 Oxygen

Phenol - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 25 Carbon, 25 Oxygen

Hydroxide - MIM
25 Hydrogen, 25 Oxygen

[bold]EPI - JM[/bold]
50 Phenol, 50 Acetone, 50 Chlorine, 50 Hydroxide → Jug

[bold]OCULINE - MIM[/bold]
20 Salt, 20 Blood, 20 Hydrogen, 20 Oxygen 

[head=2]Cryo Chem+[/head]

Prep for later... CMD
150 Water (if not from earlier), 30 Phosphorus, 30 Radium, 60 Lithium, 10 Sugar

Tricordine - DM
10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Sugar, THEN 10 Silicon, 10 Potassium, 10 Nitrogen → MD

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

Dexalin - MIM
1 Plasma, 50 Oxygen, Twice

[bold]CRYOX - MIM[/bold]
50 Dexalin, 50 Oxygen, 50 Water, Thrice (Jug 180)

Mutagen - MIM 
30 Chlorine, 30 Phosphorus, 30 Radium

[bold]DOXA - JM[/bold]
90 Cryoxadone, 90 Mutagen → Jug

60 Cryoxadone, 60 Lithium, 60 Tricordine → Jug 

Deso - MIM
Ephedrine, Carbon, Iodine, Phosphorus

70 Blood, 70 Cryox, 70 Deso, 70 Diph → Jug 110

30 Necro, 60 Cryox, 15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Radium, 60 Hydrogen

15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Oxygen, 45 Carbon → Jug

## Sheet name: Chemisty100u
What you're making,Key,What you're doing to make it,Total units,Color Label
"                       Hi! I'm Licks-the-Vials. This is a workflow guide optimized to aspiring chemists for starlight for 100u beakers.
                       
                       I recommend to coordinate with your fellow chemists to divide resources and complete the full workload. 
                       This means claiming categories of chemicals BEFORE you start working, even though we need to be fast!
                       ""Brigmed Chems"" mean a set of the colored reagents for Basic & Airloss Tier 1s and Brute, Burn, Toxin Tier 2s 
                       
                       Your beaker retains heat, there is no need to re-heat it for every recipe, you can heat it quickly with 1u on a hotplate
                       Reductive reactions (bruizine, acetone) also heat your beaker, while reductive reactions (dex) cool it.
                       You can also cool your beaker by adding room tempature, non generated chems from the dispenser, or water/welding fuel.
                       
                       Shorthand Key: (DM) = Dispenser Mix  |  (CMD) = Chem Master Dump  |  (MIM) = Mix in Master  |  (JM) = Jug Mix",,,Copy to a Nano Task paper:

## Sheet name: GoldBeakerGuide 200U
,,"Heya! Malice here! This guide is made for those that prefer using the chem master and assumes you are using the golden beaker (240u) or are mixing inside jugs. hopefully i made this new player friendly enough that everyone can follow it easily. If a recipe requires heat, preheat your beaker with 1u of plasma inside for a few seconds and empty it before starting.","tips and tricks:
1) your beaker retains heat, there is no need to re-heat it for every recipe.
2) filling your beaker with water/fuel from a tank or sink cools it to room temperature.
3) expanding reactions (dex) cool your beaker down, reducing reactions (amoxla) heat it.
4) don't forget to pull chemicals from the dispenser even when you don't need them yet.
5) coordinate, pick a category and let your fellow chemists know that you are making it.
6) alt click the master/dispenser to quickly pull the beaker out
7) experiment, everyone will have their prefered method, once you've learned the ropes try not to blindly follow mine"
,,"Start the shift by pulling 200 iron, 100 sugar, 800 oxygen, 600 hydrogen 600 carbon and 100 sillicon from the dispenser, this can be skipped if you wish but it generally helps speed you up. 
Continue by pulling out the jugs from the dispenser and putting their contents into the master by dragging and dropping the jugs onto it. 
Take the following jugs from the chemvend depending on what you will be making: 
Airloss:  1 iron. Brutes: 1 carbon, 1 sugar. 
Burns: 1 sillicon, 1 iron, 1 copper, 1 phosphorous. Poisons: 1 nitrogen, 1 sillicon. "

## Sheet name: OldSpeedrun
"                       Hi! I'm Licks-the-Vials. This is a workflow guide intended to optimize the speed of a base set of useful chemicals. 
                       This order is optimized for a Silver Beaker (150u capacity) without dumping the dispenser chem jugs.
                       THIS METHOD WORKS FINE, but due to starlights constant changes with Brigmed, create your own order!
                       Start with a syringe and get 90u of your blood in a jug whilst simultaneously dispensing these jugs:
                       3 Empty, 3 Carbon, 1 Chlorine, 1 Copper, 2 Hydrogen, 1 Iron, 2 Oxygen, 2 Silicon
                       Master Dump the Plasma vial and Carbon, Hydrogen, Oxygen, Silicon, Copper jugs, then proceed.
                       Shorthand Key: (DM) = Dispenser Mix  |  (CMD) = Chem Master Dump  |  (MIM) = Mix in Master  |  (JM) = Jug Mix",,,Copy to a Nano Task paper: 
,,,"This is a workflow guide intended to optimize the speed of producing a base set of useful chemicals.
 
This is optimized for a Silver Beaker (150u capacity) and without dumping Jugs from the Chem Dispenser.

Start with a syringe and siphon 90u of your blood into an empty jug whilst simultaneously dispensing these jugs:

3 Empty, 2 Carbon, 1 Chlorine, 1 Copper, 2 Hydrogen, 1 Iron, 2 Oxygen, 2 Silicon

Master Dump the Plasma vial and Carbon, Hydrogen, Oxygen, Silicon, Copper jugs, then proceed. You can put this paper in your pocket for easy viewing.

Shorthand Key: 
(DM) = Dispenser Mix
(CMD) = Chem Master Dump
(MIM) = Mix in Master  
(JM) = Jug Mix

[head=2]Start Mixing Here[/head]

[bold]DYLO - DM[/bold]
30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen, Twice → Jug

[bold]DERM - DM[/bold]
20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Oxygen, 40 Phosphorus, Twice → Jug & CMD overfill

Prep for Later... - CMD 
90 Lithium, 140 Sodium

30 Derm for Later... DM
5 Silicon, 5 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Phosphorus → CMD

Ammonia - DM
75 Hydrogen, 25 Nitrogen, Twice → CMD

Prep for Later... - CMD 
60 Iron 140 Sugar

[bold]BIC - DM[/bold]
80 Carbon, 20 Oxygen, 20 Sugar, Four Times → CMD

[bold]BRUIZ - MIM[/bold]
50 Bicardine, 50 Sugar, """"50"""" Lithium, Twice → Jug

[bold]ARITH - DM[/bold]
15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Radium, → Jug, 90 Hydrogen → Jug

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

[bold]AMOX - MIM[/bold]
50 Carbon, 50 Iron, 50 Ammonia, Twice

Benzene - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon, Twice → Jug

Hydroxide - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 50 Oxygen → Jug

[bold]LACE - MIM[/bold]
100 Bicardine, 100 Benzene

[bold]PNCT - MIM[/bold]
100 Bicardine, 100 Hydroxide

Ferscilite - MIM
50 Iron, 50 Silicon, Twice

[bold]LEPO - MIM[/bold]
1 Plasma, 50 Fersilicite, 50 Copper, Four Times

Dexaline - MIM
1 Plasma, 40 Oxygen

[bold]DEXP - JM[/bold]
60 Dexalin, 60 Carbon, 60 Iron → Jug

Prep for Later... CMD
200 Chlorine from Jug

Salt - MIM 
100 Sodium, 100 Chlorine

[bold]SALINE - JM[/bold]
40 Salt, 160 Water → Jug

Sodium Carb - MIM
10 Ammonia, 10 Salt, 10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen → Jug

Sodium Hydroxide - MIM
10 Hydrogen, 10 Oxygen, 20 Sodium → Jug

[bold]SIGY - JM[/bold]
40 Sodium Carbonate, 40 Sodium Hydroxide, 20 Silicon, 20 Carbon, 40 Sugar, 40 Water

[bold]PYRA - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 60 Dermaline, 60 Carbon → Jug

[bold]INSUZ - JM[/bold]
60 Leporazine, 30 Carbon, 90 Silicon → Jug

[bold]TRIC - DM[/bold]
30 Oxygen, 30 Carbon, 30 Sugar → Jug   30 Silicon, 30 Potassium, 30 Nitrogen → Jug

[head=2]Welding Fuel Chem[/head]

COOL your beaker!
Any cool liquid, Welding Fuel is great for this. Grab at least 75 Fuel and CMD

Prep for Later... CMD 
200 Carbon & Oxygen & Ethnaol from a new Jug or the Dispenser. Dispense 5 additional jugs.

Oil (@520k Oil Ashes) - MIM
50 Welding Fuel, 50 Hydrogen, 50 Carbon

WARM your beaker
Specifically use 5u Oil and heat between 400K and 500K (4 seconds on the hot plate)

[bold]DIPH - JM[/bold]
20 Ammonia, 60 Ethanol, 40 Oil, 40 Salt → Jug THEN 40 Carbon → Jug 

Ephedrine - MIM
10 Oil, 5 Ammonia, 5 Ethanol, 10 Hydrogen, 10 Sugar

Acetone - MIM
25 Oil, 25 Welding Fuel, 25 Oxygen

Phenol - MIM
50 Hydrogen, 25 Carbon, 25 Oxygen

Hydroxide - MIM
25 Hydrogen, 25 Oxygen

[bold]EPI - JM[/bold]
50 Phenol, 50 Acetone, 50 Chlorine, 50 Hydroxide → Jug

[bold]OCULINE - MIM[/bold]
20 Salt, 20 Blood, 20 Hydrogen, 20 Oxygen 

[head=2]Cryo Chem+[/head]

Prep for later... CMD
150 Water (if not from earlier), 30 Phosphorus, 30 Radium, 60 Lithium, 10 Sugar

Tricordine - DM
10 Carbon, 10 Oxygen, 10 Sugar, THEN 10 Silicon, 10 Potassium, 10 Nitrogen → MD

HEAT your beaker!
1u of anything for 5 seconds on a hot plate. The rest of the order assumes a Hot beaker

Dexalin - MIM
1 Plasma, 50 Oxygen, Twice

[bold]CRYOX - MIM[/bold]
50 Dexalin, 50 Oxygen, 50 Water, Thrice (Jug 180)

Mutagen - MIM 
30 Chlorine, 30 Phosphorus, 30 Radium

[bold]DOXA - JM[/bold]
90 Cryoxadone, 90 Mutagen → Jug 

[bold]ARCRYO - JM[/bold]
60 Cryoxadone, 60 Lithium, 60 Tricordine → Jug 

Deso - MIM
Ephedrine, Carbon, Iodine, Phosphorus

[bold]NECRO - JM[/bold]
70 Blood, 70 Cryox, 70 Deso, 70 Diph → Jug 110

[bold]CHRON - JM[/bold]
30 Necro, 60 Cryox, 15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Radium, 60 Hydrogen 

[bold]ETHYL - JM[/bold]
15 Silicon, 15 Potassium, 15 Nitrogen, 45 Oxygen, 45 Carbon → Jug 

[bold]HALOPER - DM[/bold]
COLD BEAKER!  20 Aluminum, 20 Chlorine, 20 Flourine, 20 Oil, 10 potassium, 10 iodide

[bold]CHARCOAL - JM[/bold]
Carbon, Ash as needed

Chem for Kobolds Introduction,Wizden,"[color=#0091ff][head=2]Chem for Kobolds Introduction[/head][/color]

Chemicals are powerful reagents that cause a variety of effects when metabolized. This edition of the Chemistry for Kobold series introduces various concepts and guidelines to use when creating chemicals.

[color=#0091ff][bold]STANDARD EXPECTATIONS[/bold][/color]
Every Medbay is unique; always consult with seniors. 
Most will expect the following chemicals to be made:

[bold]Basics (within 10 minutes):
• [color=#ffaa00]BIC        | Brute[/color]
• [color=#216b85]DERM   | Burn (except Caustic)[/color]
• [color=#6745c6]DYLO    | Poison[/color]
• [color=#bd5902]ARITH   | Radiation[/color]
• [color=#0064c8]SALINE | Bloodlevel[/color]
• [color=#4da0bd]DEXP     | Airloss + Bloodloss[/color]
• [color=#89f77f]AMOX   | Avali Airloss + Bloodloss[/color]

Advanced (within 30 minutes):
• [color=#ff3636]BRUIZ   | Blunt[/color] 
• [color=#788282]LACE     | Slash[/color] 
• [color=#b9bf93]PUNC    | Pierce[/color] 
• [color=#ff7db5]LEPO     | Cold [/color] 
• [color=#aa4308]PYRA     | Heat [/color] 
• [color=#8147ff]INSUZ   | Shock [/color] 
• [color=#e0a5b9]SIGY      | Caustic [/color] 

Cryochemicals:
• [color=#0091ff]CRYOX  | Cure-all (except cellular) when alive[/color]
• [color=#32cd32]DOXA    | Cellular when alive[/color]
• [color=#86a5bd]NECRO  | Cure-all (except cellular) when dead[/color]

Others, as requested:
• [color=#64ffe6]DIPH     | Poison [/color]
• [color=#ba7d7d]TRANX  | Stop Bleed[/color] 
• [color=#2d5708]ETHYL   | -Dizzy + Drunk[/color] 
• [color=#27870a]HALOP  | -Stims + Causes Sleep[/color] 
• [color=#efefef]EPI         | Cure-all when critical [/color] 
• [color=#00e5ff]TRIC      | Cure-all when healthy (<50 Damage)[/color] 
• [color=#999999]OCULINE    | Eyesight[/color] 
• [color=#4b5154]CHARCOAL | Clean Blood [/color] 
[/bold]
During this, specialized medications like botany and combat may be requested. See the other Chemistry for Kobold editions for the list of those chemicals. 

[color=#0091ff][bold]METHODS OF MIXING CHEMICALS[/bold][/color]
Most chemists will [bold]prioritize speed[/bold]. Depending on the mixture requirements, the method of mixing should change between using the Chem Master, Chem Dispenser, or within empty Jugs to achieve results.

Many Chemists elect to dump every chemical into the Chem Master and sort the chemicals alphabetically. This is OK, especially when working collaboratively, though the risks are high and [bold]all is lost[/bold] should the master be destroyed with improper mixtures or external forces.

As such, the Chemistry for Kobold editions [bold]recommend to maintain the Chem Dispenser[/bold] through refills, and dispense chemicals as-needed for a workflow.

Certain mixtures like Phal, Arith, Dylo, Bic, Derm are[bold] easy and fast to make[/bold] using the Chem Dispenser. Otherwise Jug mixing and Chem Master mixing is faster and easier when using Heat Manipulation.

[color=#0091ff][bold]HOW HEAT MANIPULATION WORKS[/bold][/color]
[bold]Heat doesn't change over time[/bold] and Beakers, Vials, Bottles and Syringes (containers) will maintain their heat indefinitely [bold]until a new liquid is put in[/bold] or there is a reductive mixture. 

This means putting room temperature water in any [bold]empty[/bold] container will cause container to be at room temperature even when emptied.

Putting new liquid into a container will combine the temperature of the mixed liquids, unless it is done using the Chem Master.

[bold]The Chem Master dispenses chemicals at the same tempature as the container inside it.[/bold]

Because of this, many Chemists will [bold]heat 1u of liquid in a `Hot` beaker[/bold] to 1000K+ in [bold]10 seconds[/bold], then keep the `Hot` beaker in the Chem Master for making heat-based mixtures.

[color=#0091ff][bold]WHAT IS A REDUCTIVE MIXTURE?[/bold][/color]
When making chemicals that reduce the overall units of the liquid, the [bold]heat increases.[/bold] 

A common example of this is Benzene, where 2 units of liquid become 1 in the mixture.

This change of temperature can occur anywhere, [bold]including in the Chem Master.[/bold] This is why a beaker can be accidentally overheated when making chemicals like Diph. This can also be utilized to gradually heat up a beaker from 310K to 1000K+ in a Chem Master.

[color=#0091ff][bold]WHAT IS A CATALYST?[/bold][/color]
A catalyst is sometimes necessary for certain chemicals. The catalyst reagent [bold]is not consumed[/bold] in the mixture and any amount, such as 1u will work.

[color=#0091ff][bold]HOW TO COLLABORATE WELL[/bold][/color]
Multiple Chemists work together and need to share resources. [bold]Ensure constrained resources are correctly distributed[/bold] for the work. Based on recommended units, each common category of chemicals [bold]need[/bold] the following Chem Vends:

• Air & Brutes | 400 Carbon, 200 Oxygen
• Burns & Toxin | 400 Carbon, 200 Oxygen, 200 Silicon
• Botany | 200 Radium, 200 Chlorine, 200 Phosphorus

When resources are constrainted, use machines to create additional chemicals, order a Chem Vend restock, or order Chemical Crates.

[color=#0091ff][bold]READ MORE IN THE REST OF THE SERIES[/bold][/color]

[bold]For more experienced chemists,[/bold] using a COLD beaker, use 30u to mix the reagents for Oil. Take only 5u of Oil and place it on a hot plate for 3 seconds. This should make a `Warm` beaker above 377K, but below 520K, before the oil ashes. If the oil ashes, try again with a COLD beaker.

This assumes you have basic knowledge and prerequisite training from Chemistry for Kobolds: Introduction.

Cryochemicals are powerful reagents but require the body to be cold, usually <213K or <150K. Typically, this is achieved through Cryogenic technology. However, freezing the body in space or cold environments also works. Some reagents only metabolize when the body is alive or dead.

To ensure a quick workflow, prepare to use plasma and 400 Oxygen, 200 Water, at least 70 Diphen. 

The process for Diphen creation is described in the Chemistry for Kobolds | Toxin. Extra Oxygen is also easily created using an Electrolizer with Water when available.","[signature]
